           │ class MarsTrafficLight(Module):                                                                                           │
           │     def locals(self):                                                                                                     │
           │         self.state = Enum(3)                                                                                              │
           │                                                                                                                           │
           │     def init(self):                                                                                                       │
           │         self.state = 0  # Start with Purple                                                                               │
           │                                                                                                                           │
           │     def next(self):                                                                                                       │
           │         if self.state == 0:  # Purple                                                                                     │
           │             self.state = 1  # Next is Orange                                                                              │
           │         elif self.state == 1:  # Orange                                                                                   │
           │             self.state = 2  # Next is Yellow                                                                              │
           │         else:  # Yellow                                                                                                   │
           │             self.state = 0  # Next is Purple                                                                              │
           │                                                                                                                           │
           │     def specification(self):                                                                                              │
           │         invariant1 = Implies(self.state == 0, self.next.state == 1)  # Purple -> Orange                                   │
           │         invariant2 = Implies(self.state == 1, self.next.state == 2)  # Orange -> Yellow                                   │
           │         invariant3 = Implies(self.state == 2, self.next.state == 0)  # Yellow -> Purple                                   │
           │         invariant4 = Or(self.state == 0, self.state == 1, self.state == 2)  # State is Purple, Orange, or Yellow          │
           │         invariant5 = Not(And(self.state == 0, self.state == 1, self.state == 2))  # Not all three states at the same time │
           │                                                                                                                           │
           │         return And(invariant1, invariant2, invariant3, invariant4, invariant5)                                            │
           │ ```                                                                                                                       │
